#### A-R157 — Shortened paths in the status bar
- **What the code does.** `truncate_path(path, 60)`: paths of 60 characters or fewer unchanged; a path of two parts or fewer → `...` + the last 57 characters; otherwise `<first part><sep>...<sep><last part>` when `60 − len(first) − len(last) − 8 > 0`, else `...<sep><last part>` — which can be LONGER than 60 when the last part is long, and doubles the separator when the first part is a root such as `C:\` (RAN TRACE T8 §7: `//.../long.xlsx`). The separator is the running system's; on Linux a Windows-style path counts as one part (RAN me s3: both 85-character examples became `...` + last 57). Used for `Source: …` and `Target: …` after a Browse.
- **Where.** `utils/helpers.py:394-416`; used `gui/handlers/file_handler.py:24, 54`.
- **Evidence.** READ; RAN (me s3; CORE exp3; TRACE T8 §7).

### 5.18 UNREGISTERED rules found while checking

The registry lists every rule above. While checking the code for this section, three further decisions turned up that the registry does not name as rules. They are described here under the label the audit conventions require; none is a GST rule.

#### U1 — UNREGISTERED: Step 3 per-file log line names the file TYPE, not the folder
- **What the code does.** After each client's copies, one line per SUCCESSFUL copy is logged: `📁 <ORIGINAL file name> → <file type> folder`, e.g. `📁 GSTR3B-Alpha Test Traders-Maharashtra-Apr.xlsx → GSTR-3B Export folder`, `… → IMS Reco folder`, `… → Annual Report folder`. The words are the TYPE text (A-R26), not the real destination (`GSTR3B`, `ITC`, the Version folder itself, A-R41), and the name is the original, not the cleaned copy name (A-R48). Failed and skipped copies get no line.
- **Where.** `gui/handlers/processing_handler.py:281-284`.
- **Evidence.** READ; RAN (TRACE T6 run 1 GUI log: `AnnualReport-… → Annual Report folder` while the file went into `Version-211026 1030`).
- **Why it matters.** A reader of the log would look for folders that do not exist; technical only.

#### U2 — UNREGISTERED: Step 4 run log and progress format
- **What the code does.** At Start the Step 4 log is CLEARED, then: a line of 60 `=`, `PROCESSING STARTED`, `Wait Time: <N> seconds`, `File Suffix: <text>`, 60 `=`; per client `📋 Processing <client> - Reports: ITC, Sales` (only the ticked ones), the processor's own lines (indented by two spaces, A-R122), then per report `✅ <ITC|Sales> Report - Refreshed successfully` (also shown when Skip Refresh merely reused a file), or `⚠️ <ITC|Sales> Report - <error>` plus `(Data extracted despite error)` when values came back, or `⏭️ <ITC|Sales> Report - Skipped` when not ticked; then `📊 Creating consolidated report...`, `✅ Processing complete!`, `📄 Report saved: <name>`, `📂 Location: <folder>`, or `❌ Error: <e>`. Each line is `[HH:MM:SS] <message>` (time taken when the event happened), coloured by tag: info `#000000`, success `#28a745`, warning `#ffc107`, error `#dc3545` (the box is Consolas 9 on `#f8f9fa`, 20 lines high, stays editable, never trimmed). Progress: client index ÷ number of clients × 100 with `Processing <client>`; 95 with `Creating consolidated report`; 100 with `Processing complete`; after the run 0 with `Ready`. (Compare the Step 3 log, A-R140, which uses different colours.)
- **Where.** `power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:875` (clear), `:888-892` (header; the two Tk values are read from the worker thread here), `:907-911`, `:918-938`, `:941-950`, `:958`, `:985`, `:1012-1029`; colours `:568-571`; box `:543-556`.
- **Evidence.** READ; RAN (PQ gui_probe with a stub processor).

#### U3 — UNREGISTERED: Order of clients in each step
- **What the code does.**
  - **Step 2 table:** clients appear in the order their FIRST recognised file appears in the scan's path-sorted list (the client dictionary keeps insertion order; A-R57 sorting).
  - **Step 3:** ticked clients are processed in table order (the selection dictionary is filled in table order).
  - **Step 4 list:** client folders appear in the order the file system lists them — no sorting (alphabetical on a Windows NTFS disk as a rule, arbitrary elsewhere: RAN me s1 and PQ on Linux showed `Beta…` before `Alpha…`).
  - **Consolidated workbook rows:** the Step 4 processing order, i.e. the Step 4 list order of the ticked clients.
- **Where.** `core/file_parser.py:233` (client created on first file), `utils/helpers.py:361` (sort); `gui/handlers/client_handler.py:30, 46, 165`; `power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:638, 688, 849`; `power_query_extractor/core/data_consolidator.py:104`.
- **Evidence.** READ; RAN (me s1; PQ scan probe).
- **Why it matters.** A rebuild that sorts clients would change the visible order of the Step 4 list and of the consolidated workbook; technical only.

The remaining Setup help texts, exactly (`gui/tabs/setup_tab.py:73-133`), because several of them describe rules and some disagree with the code:

| Key | Text | Agrees with the code? |
|---|---|---|
| `CHECKLIST_HELP['source']` | "The folder holding the GST files you received. They are read from here and copied into the target folder - nothing here is moved, renamed or deleted." | yes (A-R47, RAN TRACE T6) |
| `CHECKLIST_HELP['itc']` | "Your ITC report template (.xlsx / .xltx). A copy is made per client and its Links sheet is filled in, so the template itself is never modified." | yes for "never modified" (copies only); the checklist also accepts `.xltm`/`.xlsm` (A-R52) |
| `CHECKLIST_HELP['sales']` | "Your Sales report template (.xlsx / .xltx). Used the same way as the ITC template - copied per client, never modified." | as above |
| `CHECKLIST_HELP['target']` | "Where the organised folders and generated reports are created. Each run adds a new timestamped folder, so previous runs are left untouched." | only for Fresh runs in a new minute (A-R43) |
| `BROWSE_HELP` | "Pick the folder containing the GST files to organise" / "Pick your ITC report template file" / "Pick your Sales report template file" / "Pick where the organised output should be created" | yes |
| `WORKFLOW_HELP` | see A-R154 | "Fast - no Excel automation involved" disagrees on Windows with pywin32 |
| `PROCESSING_HELP` | see A-R43 | Resume "skipping files that are already in place" holds only within the same minute |
| `CLIENT_NAME_HELP` | "Adds the client's name to the folders inside each version folder, e.g. 'ITC (ABC Pvt Ltd)' instead of just 'ITC'. Leave off if paths are getting long - Excel stops opening files beyond about 218 characters." | real names use underscores (A-R39); 218 ⚠ (A-R104) |
| `LENGTH_HELP` | "Longest client name allowed in a folder name before it is shortened. Lower values keep paths shorter, which matters on a network share: Excel cannot open files whose full path exceeds roughly 218 characters." | applies to the Level-2 folder only (A-R36) |
| `SCAN_HELP` | "Read the source folder and work out which client each file belongs to. Read-only - nothing is copied or changed. Results appear on Step 2." | yes (the scan only reads) |
| `RESCAN_HELP` | "Clear the current results and read the source folder again. Use after adding or renaming files." | yes; it also clears the data a running Step 3 is using (A-R60) |

### 5.20 Rules that exist in several places with different values

| Concern | Place → value | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| Which Excel file extensions count | `utils/helpers.py:327` scan `.xlsx .xls .xlsm`; `utils/helpers.py:299` check `.xlsx .xls .xlsm .xltx .xltm`; patterns `utils/constants.py:15-55` `.xlsx .xls` only; `utils/constants.py:196` (dead) `.xlsx .xls .xlsm` | `.xlsm` is listed but never recognised; `.xltx`/`.xltm` pass the second check but never reach it (A-R8) |
| Which template extensions count | dialogs `gui/handlers/file_handler.py:31, 42` `*.xltx *.xlsx`; checklist `gui/utils/setup_validation.py:21` `.xlsx .xltx .xltm .xlsm`; gate `gui/handlers/file_handler.py:143-149` any; help `gui/tabs/setup_tab.py:77, 80` ".xlsx / .xltx"; dead `utils/constants.py:197` `.xlsx .xltx .xltm` | five different answers (A-R52, A-R53) |
| Excel file signatures | `utils/helpers.py:264-265` (used); `utils/constants.py:199-202` (dead) | same bytes typed twice (A-R10, A-R15) |
| Private → Pvt, Limited → Ltd | `utils/helpers.py:91-93` (underscores turned into spaces first); `utils/helpers.py:624-625` (no such step) | `X_Private_Limited` is abbreviated in file names but not in the Level-2 folder (A-R19) |
| The client name | as written (key, text report, table); abbreviated and cut to the length setting (Level-2 folder, Step 4 and consolidated "Client Name"); cleaned with underscores, full length (copies, Level-4 suffix, report names) | three forms of one name (A-R17, A-R35, A-R20) |
| State: code or text | code in the key, Level-2 folder, report names; text in the text report, table, summary, copied file names | (A-R23) |
| Type texts (`GSTR-2B Reco` …) | `utils/constants.py:68-75` and literals at `core/file_parser.py:267, 359`, `core/file_organizer.py:306-311`, `core/excel_handler.py:625, 636, 647, 661, 677, 693` | consistent today; a rename needs about 15 edits (A-R26) |
| Run folder patterns | `utils/constants.py:82, 84` (`Annual Statement-{timestamp}`, `Version-{timestamp}`); literals `power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:623, 641`; literal prefix `core/file_organizer.py:179` | same text typed three times (A-R32, A-R38, A-R42) |
| "Newest" run folder | Re-run/Resume: newest FOLDER by date modified (`core/file_organizer.py:177-184`); Step 4: newest ENTRY (files too) by date modified (`power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:623-631`) | a newer FILE named `Annual Statement-…` breaks only Step 4 (A-R42, A-R43) |
| Maximum client name length | 35 at `gui/main_window.py:93`, `core/file_organizer.py:30, 42`, `utils/helpers.py:620`, `gui/tabs/setup_tab.py:436, 614-615`, `gui/main_window.py:386-387`; arrows 15-100 (`gui/tabs/setup_tab.py:440`) | same default in seven places (A-R36) |
| Long-name warning limit | per-client toggle: fixed 10 (`gui/handlers/client_handler.py:191`); Start: the length setting, fallback 10 (`gui/handlers/processing_handler.py:92-97`) | two different thresholds for one concern (A-R136) |
| Default refreshed-file suffix | `power_query_extractor/core/report_processor.py:51`; `power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:297`; `power_query_extractor/core/refresh_naming.py:14` | same text typed three times (A-R67) |
| `{timestamp}` token | writer replaces only the exact lower-case token (`power_query_extractor/core/report_processor.py:129`); reader lower-cases the pattern first (`power_query_extractor/core/refresh_naming.py:39`) | `{TIMESTAMP}` is understood when reading but not when writing (A-R67, A-R68) |
| Settings file path | `gui/utils/cache_manager.py:15`; `power_query_extractor/extractor_main.py:41`; `power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:118` | same path typed three times; the extractor also reads three current-folder files and three extra keys nobody writes (A-R114, A-R118) |
| Wait time | default 10 at `power_query_extractor/core/report_processor.py:50` and `power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:270`; range 5-60 only on the box's arrows | a typed 0 is accepted (A-R72) |
| "Missing" text | option `power_query_extractor/config/cell_mappings.py:252`; fallback literal `'Missing'` at `power_query_extractor/core/data_consolidator.py:129, 154` | consistent today (A-R89) |
| Sheet-name matching | Links: any case, COM also "contains link" or creates one (`core/excel_handler.py:167-183`), openpyxl any case only (`:289-293`); `Info`: any case (`power_query_extractor/core/report_processor.py:646`); extraction sheets: EXACT, case-sensitive (`power_query_extractor/core/report_processor.py:565`) | three matching rules (A-R58, A-R73, A-R83) |
| Getting an Excel | Step 3: `GetActiveObject`, then `Dispatch` (`core/excel_handler.py:123-128`); Step 4: `Dispatch` twice (`power_query_extractor/core/report_processor.py:218, 536`) | different acquisition rules; neither is a private `DispatchEx` (A-R97, A-R103) |
| Opening a workbook | Step 3: one 8.3 fallback (`core/excel_handler.py:324-369`); Step 4: extended paths, close-by-name, 8.3, "all parameters" (`power_query_extractor/core/report_processor.py:687-799`); both use the 218 threshold | deliberately different (comment `utils/excel_com.py:1-15`) (A-R101, A-R102, A-R104) |
| Excel visibility | Step 3 hidden (`core/excel_handler.py:131`); Step 4 refresh visible and maximised (`power_query_extractor/core/report_processor.py:225-227`); Step 4 extraction hidden (`:537`) | (A-R97, A-R71, A-R103) |
| Time-stamp formats | `DDMMYY HHMM`, `DDMMYY_HHMM`, `YYYYMMDD_HHMM`, `YYYYmmdd_HHMMSS`, `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS`, `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM`, `HH:MM:SS` | seven formats (A-R33) |
| Log colours | Step 3: success `#4CAF50`, warning `#FF9800`, error `#F44336`, info `#2196F3`, normal `#FFFFFF` on `#1E1E1E` (`gui/tabs/processing_tab.py:106-117`); Step 4: info `#000000`, success `#28a745`, warning `#ffc107`, error `#dc3545` on `#f8f9fa` (`power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:550, 568-571`) | two palettes in one window (A-R140; 5.18 U2) |
| Wheel speed | 3 named `SCROLL_UNITS_PER_NOTCH` (`gui/tabs/setup_tab.py:138`); literal 3 (`power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:471`); log box `int(-delta/120)` without accumulation (`power_query_extractor/gui/extractor_window.py:561`) | small touch-pad movements scroll the lists but not the Step 4 log (A-R146) |
| Backup naming | real: infix `_backup_` (`utils/helpers.py:225`); dead constant prefix `backup_` (`utils/constants.py:238`); `.gitignore:54` `backup_*` | the ignore rule does not match real backups (A-R46) |
| Temporary files | real: `%TEMP%\gst_excel_temp\<report name>` (`core/excel_handler.py:53, 147, 273`); dead `temp_gst_` (`utils/constants.py:237`); `.gitignore:53` `temp_gst_*`; unused `temp/` folder created by `main.py:35` | (A-R98, A-R107, A-R128) |
| Progress-callback shapes | 3 values (`core/file_parser.py:52`, `core/file_organizer.py:226`); 4 values (`utils/helpers.py:491`, no callback ever passed) | technical (A-R141) |
| Version labels | `3.0.0` (`core/__init__.py:12`, `gui/__init__.py:11`, `utils/__init__.py:38`); `1.0.0` (`power_query_extractor/__init__.py:3`); `v3.0` in five user-visible places (A-R156) | (A-R130, A-R156) |
| Log destination | `gst_organizer.log` (`main.py:24`) or `pq_extractor.log` (`power_query_extractor/extractor_main.py:16`), both in the CURRENT folder | (A-R120, A-R121) |

### 5.21 Older documents that disagree with the rules

Every disagreement found while writing this section, in one place (each is also noted under its rule). The documents are `PROJECT_AUDIT_ANNUALSTATEMENT.md` (March 2026, A only (after the split)), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-merge-extractor-design.md` (A only (after the split)), `tests/README.md` (A only (after the split)) and `README.md` (`d674386`, shared - before the split on 30-10-2025). Appendix X4 checks every claim of these documents; this table lists only those about rules.

### 5.23 Questions for the author

These are questions only the author can answer. The GST questions ask what is intended; they do not suggest an answer.

1. **[GST RULE] Which file feeds a report** when a client has two files of one type (two years, two IMS dates, or a Windows "(1)" copy)? Today the alphabetically first full path is used and the screen does not say which (A-R57).
2. **[GST RULE] Consolidated workbook, nine columns:** for "IMS Reco", "Extra In Purchase" and "Extra In GSTR2B", are the configured cells (`IMS_Reco` AA1/AD1/AG1, `ExtraInPurchase` O1/P1/Q1, `ExtraInGSTR2B` S1/T1/U1) the ones you want to see? Today those columns show `Diff GSTR-3B` J15/K15/L15 whenever those are filled (A-R80 to A-R82, A-R88).
3. **[GST RULE] After a failed refresh**, should the figures that were still read appear (perhaps flagged), or be replaced by "Missing" as now (A-R89)? Should the copy left by a failed refresh be kept, given that Skip Refresh later reuses it as a success (A-R69)?
4. **[GST RULE] `Info!BB2`:** is it filled by Power Query during the refresh in BOTH templates (so it is empty in an unrefreshed report)? If it already holds a value before the refresh, the check cannot detect a failed refresh (A-R73).
5. **[GST RULE] Periods:** should files of different periods or financial years ever sit in one source folder for one client? The code never looks at the period part of a name (A-R25).
6. **[GST RULE] State part of file names:** what do real names contain there — full names, short forms (`MH`, `J&K`), two-digit numbers? A number crashes client selection; unknown names get invented codes that can equal a real state's code (`Unknown Place` → `UP`) (A-R17, A-R22).
7. **[GST RULE] Expected types and GSTR-3B:** are the six expected types and the "any number of GSTR-3B files" rule still current (A-R26, A-R27)? Should "Clients with Missing Files" include clients that only have duplicates (A-R31)?
8. **Templates:** which format do you use (`.xltx`, `.xlsx`, `.xltm`, `.xlsm`)? What do the Links cells contain before the program writes (empty values leave them unchanged)? Do the queries read the whole GSTR3B folder (backups and a second client's files can land there)? Which extension do the queries add to the file stems (A-R52, A-R54 to A-R57, A-R46)?
9. **Resume:** is Resume meant to continue the interrupted run's own Version folder? Today it makes a new one every minute (A-R43).
10. **Folder-name length:** should the length setting also shorten the Level-4 suffix and the report names? Values of 8 or 9 and below drop the state code (A-R35, A-R36).
11. **Client names:** do real client names contain hyphens, dots ("Co.", "Pvt. Ltd."), leading digits, or two spellings ("Private Limited" / "Pvt Ltd")? Each behaves differently today (A-R7, A-R17, A-R20, A-R35).
12. **Excel on the office PCs:** which Excel version and UI language? Does anyone keep their own Excel open while Step 3 or Step 4 runs (both may borrow and quit it)? Is the 218-character limit still the figure you see (A-R71, A-R97, A-R103, A-R104, A-R105)?
13. **Step 4 batch:** do you ever need to refresh an OLDER `Annual Statement-…` batch? Today the newest by date modified is always used (A-R42).
14. **Step 4 settings:** should the wait time, the refreshed-file suffix and Skip Refresh be remembered between sessions? Have you ever changed the suffix (A-R67, A-R119)?
15. **Share and drive letters:** do all PCs reach the output share by the same drive letter, or by `\\server\…`? The reports store absolute paths (A-R56).
16. **Logs:** where should the log live, given that it receives client names and every extracted figure and is written into whatever folder the program was started from (A-R120 to A-R122)?
